“What did it say exactly?” Charlie knew the answer and was panicking slightly herself. The fact Mimi and Carl had not heard the voice was good, but how had Anne-Marie heard it? Oh god, the dragon was still in her bag on the ground under the sofa between Anne-Marie and Charlie..

“It was scratching and hissing and some words and mathematical symbols I think.” Anne-Marie replied, thankfully ignorant to Charlie's thoughts.

They reached the library and Charlie unlocked the door.

“Mother.” She shouted into the room and felt her presence before she shouted.

“Charlie? Anne-Marie?” Her mother came up reading the whole problem directly from Charlie's mind as she arrived. Charlie listened to mothers weak thoughts below the cascade that was Anne-Marie.

“Anne-Marie, can you explain again to mother everything, exactly.”

Anne-Marie repeated the experience of the last few minutes as Charlie went back for their drinks and her bag.

[Dragon please do not contact any human entities. You are hurting the new entity. Do not acknowledge this order] She said calmly knowing it would be obeyed. Dragon was not sentient, she was, she was the entity, she controlled it.

Anne-Marie was calm when she returned, still talking to her mother. She handed Anne-Marie her glass of tea, which she sipped, still shaking a little.

[Don't be mad mother]

Charlie turned to Anne-Marie and took her hand.

“I had a similar experience, when I was twelve in the university library,” said Charlie. “It scared me to death at the time, and I went home with Mama, very upset. I soon realised how little we knew about the mind and the nature of the universe..”

“Quantum consciousness,” said Anne-Marie softly, “I had a crazy thought a few weeks ago that our anomalous signal the WOW department upstairs was trying to trace had also caused that same phenomena.”

“Well, the words ‘quantum consciousness’ themselves don’t actually mean anything,” smiled Gerty. “In fact they are in my opinion the two most overused bullshit words in all of science, and putting them together is even worse!”

“Mama is right, Anne-Marie, A good scientist always asks how and why. The university was either the source of the WOW signal, or alternatively, the consciousness was acting like just another detector.”

“Would you still be scared if we said we both heard the same thing you just did?”

[Charlie, no!]

Anne-Marie looked at Charlie, then at Gerty who were both looking at each other and not at Anne-Marie. She had not even processed the sentence yet, the stare between the mother and daughter seemed.. Uncomfortable.

[Mama, look at Anne-Marie, look into her mind. She has helped us once and there is something there, another connection with the dragon. What if this happens again, uncontrolled, without our support to help her, or in an extreme case somebody like your psychologist?]

[But Charlie, think what this is going to do to her, Anne-Marie is fragile, unprepared..]

[That is my point Mama. We can be here for her. Who else can do this? Would you have her go mad on her own not understanding, when we can be with her to help guide her through it. We can learn together]

[I.. I.. Oh Charlie, I don't know]

Gerty began to weep, not of sadness, or happiness, but for a way of life about to change. Charlie moved in and hugged her mother, looking fondly at Anne-Marie.

Anne-Marie looked on, unsure if it was her causing this.

“It’s not you who caused this Anne-Marie,” said Charlie softly. “It is you she is crying for, just as she cried for me twelve years ago. Just know you are with the two best friends you will ever have.”

Charlie picked up the tablet with the decoded journal on it.

“Let us finish the story together over there on the sofa, it is going to shock you a lot but remember I will be next to you and so will mother, one of us will always be with you.”

* * *

Anne-Marie stayed by Charlie and Gerty as they went through the journal. At no point did the two women break from their serious but concerned demeanours. After they finished the journal, Gerty took the story up to her troubled birth. Retelling the story was painful to her, and she again found herself in tears, though Anne-Marie was also in a state of shock.

“The journal, the dragon, the..” She hesitated to say the word. “The magic. It’s true then?”

“Yes.” Said Charlie stoically. “But this is the beginning of the story, and you and I know there is no such thing as magic, only science we do not yet understand.”

Charlie got up from the sofa still holding Anne-Marie’s hand and looking into her eyes deeply. “However we now get to the point where our current understanding of science has broken down and you have a choice to walk away or to stay with us on the journey and help us uncover what is really happening.”

“There is however one thing you need to know,” said Gerty seriously. “You have already crossed a line in the sand. What you know so far is dangerous enough to get you or all of us killed, or even worse put in cages like lab rats should you mention it to anyone. Regardless of which path you choose this will have to always remain a secret between the three of us..”

“..And no,” added Charlie, “not even Michael can know, because even knowing the information is enough to get him killed as well. We will find a way to get this information safely to the world eventually, but it needs to be under terms and conditions which keep us all safe.”

Gerty continued. “Remember Anne-Marie, we may have a lot of power in Switzerland, but ours is a tiny country surrounded by large predators, any of which would not hesitate to take the information you know by force, and they most certainly will not use it for the betterment of mankind.”

“Do you understand?”

Anne-Marie thought deeply for at least thirty seconds, processing the data logically. Then she looked at Charlie.

[You can really read my mind, can't you?] thought Anne-Marie.

“Yes, I can read your surface thoughts,” said Charlie. “As can my mother. You need to be very close though, and no crowds. It is how we do our job, how we help, and why there are no other negotiators. It is hidden behind these Sherlock Holmes, mentalist-like insights which are fake but have enough credibility to be believed.”

“So you know my answer is yes then.” Anne-Marie said smiling at both of them for the first time since she arrived.

They both hugged her.

“This next part is going to be a bit scary,” said Charlie. “The thing you heard and saw in your head was the artefact called the dragon. I have been hearing it since I was twelve, and my mother only heard it for the first time today. Up until I read the diary yesterday, I had diagnosed it as a kind of split personality disorder in my mind. We also think there are two of them and they are the source of the WOW anomaly CERN and Fermi-lab may be looking for..”

“Before we go any further Charlie,” said Gerty, “it is nearly two o'clock, you need to postpone a meeting with a certain American Interpol agent, and I need to get us all a serious amount of chocolate and a refill for my coffee.”

* * *

Anne-Marie shrank back a little when Charlie pulled out the dragon from her bag.

“We believe it is safe,” Gerty said, “we have checked for radiation and also done a simple check for electromagnetic emissions, but there was nothing we could detect. But we also need to remember it is very, very, advanced technology, and like any advanced technology we have no idea, so we must take this slowly and carefully.”

Charlie went through what she saw when she communicated with the dragon a few hours earlier, Anne-Marie’s face getting more and more excited as she tried to ask scientific questions that Charlie had no answers for.

“We know from the journal that this can be extremely dangerous. This and the other one have probably killed thousands of people throughout history. But it seems that danger is either a defensive response or a command given by a sentient entity, that is you, me, or Mama.”

“I know it has some rules built in, like an alien version of Asomov’s three laws of robotics, the first rule seems to be the same as his, in that it will not harm an entity. I think you, me, and Mama are therefore safe. But there are other people in the house and closeby and whatever triggers this, we know that walls are not an obstacle..”

“Ask it!” Anne-Marie said, “you keep treating it in the third-person when it is here and available to answer questions.”

Charlie shrugged, grabbed the dragon and immediately spoke.

“Yes, it does not consider the others sentient if they cannot communicate with it, you are not sentient until it has a map of your [...] entity [...] Damn, er, some kind of imprint which it creates during contact.”

She offered the dragon to Anne-Marie.

“No it will not harm you,” said Charlie. “Consider it a kind of scan, though why it needs physical touch when it operates on another dimension and through another force which transcends matter I do not know..”

“Sorry I do know,” she said immediately realising she could just ask the question. “It is a pattern identifier in this universe which it needs to encode on a specific neuron type is my best guess, the longer it has to encode this the stronger the connection. It will try to convert normal neuron cells but if they are already formed it may destroy the cell.”

“I have no idea what you are talking about sweetie, are you basically saying that the longer you hold it the better your connection gets?” said Gerty.

“Or you die!” Said Charlie to concerned faces from both women.

“OK I need to check some more.” Again she went quiet for a second opening her eyes a little more slowly like she was tired.

“It recognises all three of us and we all have the correct neuron types so yes we are all safe.”

“You look so tired sweetie,” said her mother.

“It is tiring, yet I felt good after only an hour of rest and that bar of chocolate, do you think I could get some more?”

Gerty smiled, “There must truly be a god if I now have an excuse to eat chocolate every day.”

Anne-Marie reached over apprehensively to Charlie. The two women watched as she took it, and her thoughts disappeared. Anne-Marie sat still for a few seconds, then her whole body collapsed into the sofa, still holding the dragon.

Charlie immediately grabbed it off her.

“I can hear her thoughts, REM sleep,” said Gerty. “She is just sleeping I think.”

[What did you do!] said Charlie to the dragon.

[Entity Anne-Marie went through the] [...] [...] [first level until she disconnected]

[Explain?]

[level one teaching was initiated by entity Anne-Marie] [disconnected at four nine five five eight time units]

“This is one of the concepts I failed to understand, Mama. Unless you are specific, a dragon does not have a standard concept of time like us. A time unit for it is the shortest time it takes to deliver a packet of information to an entity. Let me check..”

Gerty felt Charlie’s mind drop out for a tiny fraction of a second.

“OK. Dragon said she was doing some sort of training course. I would say poor Anne-Marie was doing it for nearly fifteen hours in dragon time. Her mind must be exhausted I think.”

[dragon, can you limit the connection to one thousand of your time units. Human entities require sleep to function, this is what happened with entity Anne-Marie]

[I can if you instruct me and she does not override your instruction]

[Do it please for all human entities effective immediately]

“Well, I am at least glad she had a large piece of chocolate cake,” said Gerty. “Let’s get her to a guest bedroom to rest.”

“No, I think a strange room will frighten her, I will go get a blanket and some pillows and we can take turns being here. I better not use the dragon as I have that meeting at six and I don't want to be tired myself.”

* * *

Charlie had switched the afternoon meeting with Ethan to six o'clock and to the dining room in the house rather than go into town. The kitchen staff at her uncle's house were used to preparing dinners at short notice and were happy to come over and help, especially as Mimi was still preparing for her exhibition.

Ethan Curran was driven from his hotel to their house by one of uncle’s chauffeurs and he arrived bang on time at six.

Charlie was curious to see what the man looked like, and a little of his background and had cheated by checking the Interpol personnel database a few days after the meeting was arranged. Ethan had done the same, getting a very filtered subset of the woman now before him.

“Good evening Ethan, I apologise for the last-minute re-arrangements of the time and venue, I hope I can make it up to you with dinner,” she said pleasantly in French, holding out her hand to shake.

She left mother with Anne-Marie in the library, grabbed an hour's sleep then showered, performed her usual ritual on her hair and changed into a more official and elegant dress. The man was also well dressed, a light Italian suit and shoes, a hand taller than her and fit looking, but it was his face she found fascinating, she had never seen green eyes before and they were hard not to focus on. Neither was he wearing the glasses from his Interpol profile picture.

“Good evening to you Miss Schneider..”

“Charlie remember!” She interrupted as he took her hand for a short handshake.

“..Charlie, it is a beautiful place you have here, I really didn't expect it to be so palatial. And you yourself look quite amazing if I may be so bold.”

His French was average, but he was definitely a little out of practice.

“Why thank you Ethan, you can be as bold as you like as long as they are all compliments.” She switched to English. “However while your French is quite good I suspect my English is much much better and I would rather you not be on the back foot during any of our discussions. Also the waiting staff do not speak English. Is this acceptable to you?”

God this woman was amazing he thought, and Charlie smiled.

“Yes, English would be preferable, I can get by but I am certainly out of practice.”

“Don't demean yourself,” she said, escorting him to the dining table which had been laid out with four places, in case mother decided to join or even Anne-Marie. “Practice makes perfect and I do believe you have a few other languages under your belt, so you’re certainly heads and shoulders above most of the people who I meet.”

She sat at the head of the table and put him directly to her left so they were close. A serving woman came out of nowhere with a bottle of her cousin's best wine.

“Can I tempt you with a local wine or would you like something a bit more.. American?” She smiled playfully.

“Come now Charlie, Americans are not as bad as your media make them out to be, and the wine I am sure will be excellent.”

“Touche!” She said, thinking this man wasn't going to be a pushover, she needed to up her game a little.

She thanked the serving lady after she had finished pouring and moved off. The house staff at her uncles were the best in the world, they knew when to come over, a brief look or a nod was all that it took and they left as soon as their task was finished, never hanging around. She preferred the informality of Mimi but also appreciated the upper class game when it was played correctly.

“It seems we have a common enemy in the group called The ElderNacht,” she said without preamble. “You need to know exactly what I know and if there was anything I missed out of the report I sent.”

Ethan was caught a little off guard. The woman was young, charming, sophisticated but her directness surprised him.

“Yes. I suspect they murdered the three men to cover up something..”

“Three men? There were two murders in Munich, the other was critical but stable, has he now died?”

Damn, he was hoping not to mention Crane.

“No Charlie..”

He was trying to delicately frame what she already knew.

“..There was another man in San Francisco. A, Mr Crane, a hired assassin. We caught him at the airport, but he was murdered before the agency could question him.”

“In CIA custody, so there is a mole in the CIA too?”

He thought back to what he just said, was there some nuance..

“I never said he was in CIA custody.”

Hmm, smart, she would need to be careful of this one.

“You said agency, and as you are currently seconded to the CIA from Interpol I made the simple leap. My uncle Frank is head of Interpol in Lyon, I am also an uncommitted Interpol agent, remember our phone call?”

“Yes sorry, he was murdered in CIA custody, we do believe his target in San Francisco airport was you, and was to frame your murder on the CIA. I believe it was because you recently were present at the Munich interviews.”

“Highly likely. But the bad guys rarely come after negotiators, just like they rarely come after policemen, especially if they are one hundred percent guilty. They only come after you if they think you know something they don't want you to know and that means there is more to the story I am not yet seeing.”

“What is an uncommitted Interpol agent?” He said in a nice segue.

“Simply that I am considered an Interpol agent when I need to be, as in when I was investigating The ElderNacht and I needed access to their records.” Charlie paused and faintly nodded to the server who came over with soup.

“That was quite an impressive report you wrote, I don't think I have seen one so summarised and accurate.”

“Why thank you Ethan, and I guess I should also say thank you for saving my life in San Francisco. I assume it was you?”

Ethan thought back to the wink she gave the one way screen and gave her the briefest of smiles before he took a sip of the soup.. Wow it was good, even better than his moms!

“This soup is really good, Charlie,” he said, breaking off a small bit of bread.

“I will let Francois, my uncle's Chef, know when I see him next.” She smiled a sexy smile and got the response she expected. So he was a little attracted to her. Nice to know.

“Regarding the summary, I built a new large language model trained on the information I had in order to get the information. I still have it and I am happy to give it to the CIA so they can add their own database to it and see if the summary changes.”

“You built a..”

“Oh yes sorry, I can get a bit techy at times. That is why I was in California to upgrade my computer skills, the new AI model was a spin-off from my training. It was quite a task to separate the wheat from the chaff, and The ElderNacht has its fingers in a lot of bad pies.”

“Horrible metaphor, but I do agree. And thank you. I am not as tech savvy as most, but I will get your program to the correct people who do know these things and I will share with you any new information I get.”

They made a few more pleasantries as their bowls were removed and their glasses topped up. Then new plates were put in front of them and a big dish put between them, the server removed the lid.

“Spaghetti and meatballs! You gotta be kidding me, my favourites.”

“The chef seems to have found this recipe online from a, Helga Curran, it seems she has a little YouTube channel. Plus there is no word in the French language for burger and fries.”

Charlie smiled as did Ethan at the little joke.

“Helga Curran is my mom, you went online and found my favourite recipe!” His smile was infectious, “God Charlie, I could kiss you!”

“Now, now, Ethan, let’s not get ahead of ourselves, firstly the chef did, and secondly my mother is probably in the other room and doesn't take kindly to strange men kissing her daughter.”

Then he reached for the bowl and helped himself to a large helping, passing the shallow bowl over to her after.

[Oh my god, this is delicious] She read his thoughts but his expression was giving the same information in buckets.

Charlie took a smaller portion and tried them.

“My, they are quite nice, I guess we have two chefs to thank for this one.”

She dabbed her nose with her napkin as a little bit of sauce flicked onto it. “Glad I didn't wear white, I guess eating meatballs is an art-form.”

“Now regarding tomorrow,” Charlie said.

“Sorry, yes, back to the case. Er, tomorrow..”

“The Bern museum has a lot of un-scanned information on the Nazi occupation of Italy and especially on the Nazi occultist who worked in Rome who looks to have started The ElderNacht. We will be driving there at ten AM, it is almost a two hour drive from here and you are welcome to join me or you can do your own investigations here and wait to see my summary.”

“We?”

“My Bodyguard and Driver Simon. He wants to talk to his counterparts in the FIS.”

“The FI..” His look of confusion resolved immediately. “Oh yes the Swiss secret service, it is sometimes easy to forget even small stable countries have intelligence agencies.”

She smiled.

“We also have a few other diplomatic resources we can call on in the Caribbean. The French Foreign Office keeps its eyes on that particular island, and The ElderNacht especially, it seems they like to sow the seeds of rebellion in the surrounding populations. The French diplomatic service also owes my mother a big favour which I intend to collect on while I am in the Capital.”

“If I was looking for the best outcome though, I think our best bet is to directly question one of The ElderNacht members themselves.”

“That last one may take time to arrange, but as far as the trip to Bern goes I will accompany you Charlie, firstly because I am concerned you are still in danger. and secondly we can discuss this more on the journey.”

She nodded. “Then we are settled, Ethan. No more business talk this evening, tell me a bit more about yourself..”

* * *

Ethan left at eight thirty as it was getting dark, she thanked the staff and wandered back into the library wondering why neither Gerty nor Anne-Marie showed up. She walked in to find both sleeping on the large sofa, the dragon still in her mothers hand.

She took the dragon carefully out of her mothers hands so as not to wake her and checked Anne-Marie who was still sleeping contently.

[dragon, please relate last activity with entity Gerty]

[learning mode eight thousand time units in eight separate sessions as per instructions]

Oh Mama! She thought shaking her head as she went upstairs to get more bedding and pillows. She then called Simon to help her move another sofa into the room, and he helped move her mother carefully onto it.

“Sweet dreams,” she said quietly, thanked Simon, went to the kitchen to get a hot chocolate then went over to the last remaining comfy chair in the library.

She picked up the dragon from behind the cushion where she had hidden it from Simon while he was here, sat down heavily, and looked at it. So innocuous, what marvels were packed into this tiny structure she would never understand. Yes she was very smart, but not as smart as others such as Anne-Marie, who had worked all their lives in a particular field. She also knew that memory capacity did not directly relate to processing capacity, most of the human population were now living proof of that, just give a stupid person a smart phone!

She wondered briefly about bringing Simon, Carl and Mimi in on it too, all were trustworthy, and probably deserved to know the danger they were possibly in, but it felt like a step too far.

[dragon what do you know about The ElderNacht]

[insufficient information]

Hmm [OK dragon, tell me about the second dragon, are you in contact with it?]

[...] [processing element] [...] [disconnected from entity] [contactable]

[what is the location of] [the second dragon?]

[...] [...] [...] [...]

How to do distance, maybe do one of those training courses that Gerty and Anne-Marie covered.. No, that would take too long. I need the information now.

[what is the physical size of this unit?] a bit loose but..

[...]

[in French]

[in what dimension?] replied the dragon.

Huh! God this was infuriating! [The dimension of length..]

[not understood]

OK no that wasn't right, OK a little assumption [the first dimension]

[four seven three] [..]

OK, this might work.

[equate point oh six five metres to the size of this unit and the dimension name to length]

It stayed quiet, she shrugged, here goes nothing.

[how distant is the second dragon in the dimension length and the unit metres]

[five seven eight three five two nine metres]

Oh wow, she ran over to her world map, grabbed some pencil and string. It didn't line up on Costa Verde as she expected, it was a bit short, covering most of the east coast of the USA, central Africa, India, China, all the way up to the north pole.. Damn it could still be anywhere, she really needed a direction!

Charlie sat in her chair thinking about how to do direction. The obvious answer was to get another reading from a different position but the dragon was dangerous and she didn’t want to take it anywhere it could be lost, stolen or damaged.

Wait, oh stupid! She looked around and found the big globe next to another bookcase. Damn! The distance the dragon would have given was the euclidean distance, straight through the earth, not the distance along the surface of the earth. She ran back to her computer and ran the conversion then drew the new slightly longer distance over the surface of the earth. It bisected exactly the east coast of Costa Verde, across the land owned by The ElderNacht. It wasn't conclusive by any means but it did fit some current facts.

[What is the status of the second dragon]

[...] [...] [...] [...]

Oh god, this would never get easy she thought as the strange symbols, crackles, hisses and mathematics crashed through her mind.

[Stop!] Now how to phrase this.. [how many human minds are within one thousand metres of the second dragon?]

[there are three hundred and twenty seven humans]

[How many human entities has the second dragon communicated with?]

[nineteen entities none successful]

[How many units of time was the last attempted communication?]

[eighty six thousand two hundred and seven units]

So a few days. So they know about the dragon and they are trying to get control of it. God she also really needed to take a small amount of time to give it human units of measure.

[Can you shut down the second dragon]

[no]

[Can you block the second dragon from any further human communication attempts?]

[no]

Well it was worth a try. But they will get lucky sometime. She and Gerty were special but there were obviously other outliers like Anne-Marie who could gain control of it. She dreaded the thoughts of what an organisation such as The ElderNacht had planned for when that happened.

Wait what happened to the others who..

[dragon, what is the status of the nineteen human entities that attempted communication with the second dragon]

[their identifications no longer exist]

[dead, you mean?]

[...] [...] [dead]

Ouch, so nineteen people died, this was bad. God she was getting tired too, just talking to the dragon really took it out on her. At least she did not need to physically hold it any longer, it seemed the short time they had been communicating closely was long enough for it to do whatever it did to get familiar with her. She put it back in the vault and sealed it.

Charlie spent a short time giving the dragon some more units of measure, like kilometres, hours, minutes, she knew they would be useful later then she looked at her mother and Anne-Marie asleep and decided to call it a night herself. She thought for a moment about sleeping on the chair, but decided on her bed setting an alarm for 8 a.m. just in case, though she was sure Simon would wake her as they had to pick up Ethan at his hotel at nine thirty.

# An Act of Desperation

ANNE-MARIE AND MAMA were still sleeping when she entered the library at eight-fifty AM. She turned the main lights on, overriding the dim desktop light she had left on in the corner overnight, and saw slight movements from both women.

Gerty awoke first, Anne-Marie moved a little then settled back down snoozing.

“Hi Mama, wakey, wakey!” Charlie said spiritedly.

“Huh..” Grunted Gerty.

“It is almost 9 a.m. Mama! Mimi is just getting some strong coffee. You and Anne-Marie crashed out last night and I decided to leave you both here, Anne-Marie should be awake soon, and you will probably both be hungry.”

She walked up to Gerty and kissed her on the head looking at Anne-Marie. “Can you talk to CERN and get Anne-Marie excused from work for the rest of the week at least. The ElderNacht has the second dragon and are trying to control it. We are going to need all hands on deck.”

“Our dragon is back in the vault and I am going to Bern with Simon and the American Agent Ethan in twenty minutes..”

Mimi walked in with a tray, some mugs, and a big pot of percolated coffee on it.

“Oh my Gerty,” she smiled, “a bit old for sleepovers aren't we!” She slid some papers out of the way on the small table next to them and unloaded the coffee.

She looked at Anne-Marie. “Is she OK?” She said, concerned.

“Just fine,” said Gerty, “once she calmed down we ended up talking most of the afternoon and evening, I guess we just got carried away.”

“Charlie said to make pancakes with lots of honey and maple syrup, so if you can get Miss Sleepyhead awake in the next twenty minutes.. Oh and it’s raining so we are eating inside.”

Mimi turned around, grabbing some old drink glasses and plates and putting them onto the tray before leaving.

“I expect I will be back before six,” said Charlie, as she started her way out of the door. “If you could also let your person in the French embassy know I will be there this afternoon to collect on that favour, it would be good too.”

Gerty waved her off, rubbing her eyes. “Go!”

Charlie walked out of the room into the kitchen, Simon was eating a stack of about six pancakes, impossible to tell exactly because of the ample covering of maple syrup.

“Ten minutes Charlie!” He said between bites, not even looking at her.

Charlie grabbed a plate and the top pancake off the pile Mimi had made, then opened the honey and scraped lashings of the thick yellow buttery manuka on it.

“OK, let's go!” Said Simon scraping his chair as he got up eight minutes later.

* * *

The Priest watched through his binoculars from his boat five hundred metres offshore. An old fishing rod propped up to make it look like he was fishing. The operation would begin in about forty minutes, he had been told that the team would then drive to the house, capture the younger negotiator woman and kill anyone else there. The helicopter would then pick everyone up, and get them to a small airfield near Turin where she would be returned for interrogation.

The earlier rain had dulled his spirits, and he was still annoyed he wasn't considered important enough to be involved in anything other than getting the gate opened. But he would have the last laugh, incorporating it into his plan, letting The ElderNacht get the blame for the killings, and the robbery, and then providing a quick way out of the country by helicopter.

He had left the gas on in the rental accommodation before he left, and had triggered the small incendiary device when he was a few hundred metres offshore, enjoying the distant pop and wisp of smoke. Let the police and firemen try and find anything from the ashes!

As he was deliberating, and screwing the silencer onto his gun, his eyes caught the tiny black Mercedes driving off in the distance. Probably one of the negotiators, hopefully the young one, and the black bodyguard. He didn't care, it wasn't his operation any more, and if The ElderNacht thought they could do better than him then good luck to them.

He checked his watch again, still thirty nine minutes until the fireworks began, but time to begin his own plan. Twenty minutes later he clicked the small lever to engage forward gear, he eased the boat up to a gentle speed, aiming for the small pier, only a groundskeeper, a housekeeper and an old woman now stood in his way.

* * *

The large white van had been stolen from an air-conditioning contractor that morning and the back was now packed with a dozen large LPG cylinders and C4 explosives.

Matheus and three of his team followed the white van in two older white BMW’s to the back security gate, manned by two guards. Their contact had already given the correct authorisation to admit them and they passed through without any trouble or even anyone having a brief glance into the vehicles.

Their man was waiting outside the building for them at 10 a.m. as instructed. He was obviously not aware of what was about to happen, just told to provide access to the building so they could obtain a backup tape.

Matheus got out of the car, three others after him and four more from the second car, leaving just the driver of the van. All the men were dressed in the white overalls of the air conditioning company, but despite this none of them looked like contractors and the man waiting outside knew something was not right.

Matheus walked up to him.

“You have the access key?” He said in English in his thick German accent.

“Why do you need eight men to steal a tape backup..”

Matheus grabbed the man around the throat and pushed him up to the wall.

“Do you have the access key?” He said very forcefully.

The man's trembling hands reached into his lab coat and pulled out an RFID card.

“Are the WOW team all in there?”

“Yes.” His voice trembled.

The man never said another word as Matheus pulled him back and slammed his head against the concrete wall. His scream was cut off immediately and the man fell like a rag-doll, blood pouring from the wound.

“You!” He said to the nearest man, “dump him over there behind the barrels, you,” he pointed at the van driver, “get the van against that roller door and set the timer for twenty minutes. The rest of you, load up and come with me, we are out of here in ten.”

Seven men followed him through the door all armed with automatic weapons. The muted thuds of gunfire inside started a minute later.

* * *

The explosion shook the CERN complex and woke up most of the surrounding area. A massive cloud rose from the remains of the data centre and the WOW laboratory.

The two cars rolled past the security gates just seconds before the explosion and on to the D35 driving to the vineyard. The hard job was done, the next part should be easy.

Downstairs, Luc, Carlos and Michael were working at their computers. The enormous explosion was simultaneous with the power going out, and followed by several roof beams and tonnes of concrete falling from the ceiling, pipes burst and water, air and some other gases began to pour out.

A crash and a scream from Luc’s direction was the last thing Michael heard.

* * *

Charlie and Ethan occupied the back seats of the car. They had been driving for an hour going over what Charlie had recently found out. She mentioned it was an artefact and she was not specific to its function, but hinted that The ElderNacht believed it had a similar power to the Ark of the Covenant.

Ethan was of course rightly sceptical and certainly not taking it as seriously as she had hoped. Charlie was in the middle of explaining how the founder of The ElderNacht was an occultist Nazi when Ethan’s phone picked up the Interpol alert.

She caught Simon glancing back briefly through the mirror, but otherwise he had been his usual focused self since giving Ethan the once over and nodding.

“Sorry, I should have had it on silent,” he said, pulling it out of his breast pocket and looking at the message.

Charlie read what he read.

“Oh my god, there has been some sort of shooting and explosion at the CERN facility, it says here it..”

Dragon interrupted her as simultaneously she felt a wave of dread.

[Entity Gerty requests help]

“Simon. Turn around NOW!” Charlie screamed.

As she said it, Simon felt a massive wave of dread wash over him. The road was wet, and several cars, in front of him, swerved the same time he did, one on the opposite side dangerously crossing the centre line, but recovering swiftly.

His special training kicked in, he did not know what the threat was yet. Normally without further information he would have sped up not slowed down, but Simon knew who Charlie was and what she did, and quietly suspected she was a lot more than she seemed. In all his years of driving her until now she had been the perfect passenger, albeit one who preferred sitting next to him and talking too much.

Once it slowed enough the powerful Mercedes turned as gracefully as any three tonne vehicle doing sixty kilometres an hour on a wet road could, leaving an almost perfect semi-circle of black tyre marks in its wake. His course corrected, he floored the throttle and all of them were pushed back in their seats.

The road was luckily quiet, he activated his blue and red flashing lights specially fitted to his last three official vehicles but never once used, then started to remember the speed-dial process that would connect his cell phone with Carls. But Charlie was already on the phone. Ignoring Ethan trying to get her attention.

“Carl, Gerty is in trouble and may be hurt..”

“Yes, don't ask, I just know!” Her voice was powerful and assertive, one used to being obeyed when the time dictated. Simon had been present one or two times where his hand was quivering on his concealed 38 as Charlie, then a twenty one year old repeatedly used that same voice to throw allegations at men almost as big as him.

“We are on our way back, Simon is breaking every traffic regulation in Switzerland now, but maybe forty minutes.”

“Yes. Immediately!”

“Bye.”

She turned to him. “Simon, please don't ask, but something terrible has happened, I don't know what or how I just feel it. The words were coming out more and more mumbled from her, a million events in her mind vying for attention. Her emotions broke down into tears, pushing everything out but two voices.

Ethan did not have a clue what was happening, the fact they were now heading back to Geneva at an extremely scary rate of knots was good. The fact Charlie seemed to have changed completely seemed bad. He wasn't sure exactly what to say seeing her in tears like this, and did the most logical thing and put his arms softly around her.

[Tell entity Gerty I am forty minutes away and Carl has been called]

[Contact with Entity Gerty intermittent]

She quickly analysed the two events.

[dragon, how many human minds are within your immediate range?]

[five]

Damn. Mimi and Aurelian, Mother would never use the dragon to defend her and Anne-Marie, that one intruder, hopefully Carl could get there in time.

[What is the status of Entity Gerty and Entity Anne-Marie?]

[No contact with Gerty in forty eight seconds, no contact with Entity Anne-Marie in seventeen hours, thirteen minutes and twenty one seconds]

But five minds, so mother and Anne–Marie were probably still alive if there was one intruder, or maybe there were two intruders and.. She did not want to go there and more tears came.

Simon was too professional to take his attention from the road, he was flying past cars, the speedometer dangerously hovering at two hundred and twenty five on the straight road. He could somehow also feel something was wrong, but the fight or flee scenarios, chiselled into him, knew to follow orders. And the only order he or the other bodyguards had ever received for the past fifteen years was to protect Gerty and her daughter, and of course his beloved Mimi.

Both Ethan and Simon were desperate for answers. Ethan glanced at the report scrolling down through the tiny amount of information he had, it seemed to be targeted at something called the WOW facility.

“The ElderNacht was responsible for the attack. I believe my mother is their next target.”

“But how do you know?” Began Ethan.

“I just know!” She said a bit angrily and without explanation. The same sense of fear shivered through him and made him back off unsure.

Simon pressed the throttle down a little harder as the road opened up. He flew by a large truck so fast it looked to be stationary, then eased off back to a hundred and seventy, having to shift his foot from the accelerator to the brake as the regenerative braking struggled to feed back so much power back into the batteries.

A call came in and he activated the speaker immediately with a tap to the wheel control.

“Go ahead, over!”

“Carl here, ETA at the house in forty seconds, silent alarm has just been triggered, I am going in hot. Local forces alerted, ETA nine minutes behind me. Signing off. Over.”

He knew Carl well, he had been Charlie’s protection detail for the last year and had definitely saved her life on the Russian border incident. He could run faster, fight, bench press and shoot better than he could, and Simon considered him a total professional.

It was a thankless task protecting two castles that were four kilometres apart, and he was not going to insult Carl's professionalism by stating the three women were on-site defenceless. Nor was Simon going to make any stupid emotional outbursts to be careful of them. Carl was going in hot, that means he was weapons live and like Simon he would identify all targets before shooting to kill.

“Roger, ETA thirty minutes. Over and Out.”

Charlie turned to Ethan. “Carl said the wrong combination entered by my mother caused the alert. Gerty could not have physically made a mistake, we talked hypothetically on this some years back so whatever the number she entered was, it meant something.”

“Ethan, your iPad please.”

He paused a second, then gave it to her without question.

Her hands worked a browser screen for a few seconds, looking at the seemingly random numbers. “OK unfortunately there are no cameras inside of the house and the outside security system has gone off-line, but the door access control system is still active, the keypad entry has letters as well as numbers,” she said. “The code she entered was fourteen digits, 62844270372653.”

Charlie thought for maybe five seconds, “there are two million, eight hundred and thirty four thousand letter combinations..”

Ethan stared at her and the screen with the 14 digit code on open-eyed, wondering where the calculation was coming from, surely she was not doing this in her head!

Charlie missed his thoughts, her brain deep into the calculations. She had never before exhibited her abilities so blatantly to strangers, but she was past caring, Also Ethan seemed to be one of the good guys.

“OK There are forty seven possible words or phrases. The only string that matches what we currently know is the name MATHEUS FRANKE, who is a high-up member of The ElderNacht.”

Ethan was still trying to understand how she got the name, he pictured an image of the keypad on his desk phone, damn how did she do that!

The blue and red flashing lights were having their effect. Simon was too professional to compromise Charlie's safety, and he knew the limits of the car and the road even in the wet.

She moved off Ethan, wiping her eyes and nose with a hankie. “I was wondering whether it was relevant to say a minute ago, but regardless of your scepticism, The ElderNacht believes that CERN can somehow detect this artefact using one of their large detector arrays.”

She could sense his wavering, and continued more forcefully.

“You do not have to believe in a terrorist's religious beliefs or their deity, but you do have to believe they will steer passenger planes into skyscrapers if they think it is rightful for their cause.”

He snapped back to life. “I believe you. How long does it take to drive from CERN to your house?”

“Ten minutes if you don't care about traffic regulations.”

[So they may be there already] Ethan gulped, he did not want to say what Charlie already knew. He refreshed the tablet they had been using and started pulling up summaries relating to CERN.

“The Islamic martyrs have claimed responsibility..” He read off the page. “Wait, It says here that the Fermi-lab scientists arrived this week.”

“The terrorist part is bullshit!” Said Charlie, “but Anne-Marie already mentioned Fermi-lab. They have a similar project, and just this week started to combine their efforts..”

“Do you think that is why they made their move? Because they thought CERN and Fermi-lab would find this artefact?”

“Yes I believe so,” she sniffed. “But this move seems more like an act of desperation to prevent us finding out what we already know, which is that they are on the Island of Costa Verde in the Caribbean.”

Simon ignored them, concentrating one hundred percent on the road ahead. Charlie appreciated Simon. Like her, when he needed to do his job he did it effectively and no questions asked. Yet she could sense his worry for Mimi encouraging him to want to take more risks. His training also knew shaving a few seconds off the journey would also likely mean they may not get there. And he needed to get to the woman he loved.

Whatever Charlie and Gerty did, he had never seen anytime where they had not drawn the correct conclusions. There were whispers, and people speculated, but their talent was an amazing force for good. Still in some cases there were bad guys who felt aggrieved at the decisions. People went to jail, companies lost billions. The cases were thankfully few as the girls generally turned over that part to the authorities, but sometimes bad just happened.

Simon's thoughts turned briefly from the driving to ensure Charlie was OK. She was sobbing, which even he, a man who was hardened by years of training couldn't fail to acknowledge, he had known her since she was five, a daughter in everything but name. Twenty three minutes to go.

* * *

[... ] [...] [...] [...] [...]

Gerty barely heard the dragon in her head, she was in complete agony. The pain from the silenced bullet going through her right hand would have dropped her to the floor had not the gun then turned on Mimi.

[dragon] [tell] [entity Charlie] [I] [need] [help] Each thought was pushed out between the waves of pain.

She was glad she had found out how to turn off the defence function of the dragon. The winery and main highway were both under a kilometre away, and the thought of dozens, maybe hundreds of innocent people dying in such horrendous fashion as described in the journal had weighed heavy on her mind. Please Carl get here!

Mimi had been calm up until that point, she had reluctantly opened the gate, after the man had shot Gerty in the hand, and then threatened to shoot her in the head. Gerty was now having trouble processing the thoughts of two people through the pain, her dress was wrapped around her hand, and soaked in her blood. She could see Mimi’s countenance waver as the gun was suddenly turned from Gerty’s head to hers..

“The vault,” he said to Gerty, “or China Girl here gets the next bullet through her skull.”

His thoughts were pure evil. They told her that he had already shot the old groundskeeper, and would keep the Chinese woman alive long enough to get in the vault and convince the old woman that she had a chance. But they were all going to die, neither he, nor The ElderNacht wanted any witnesses.

There was another ten minutes until The ElderNacht arrived to see what a good job he had done. Just ten minutes to fill his backpack with the loot from that vault, then he would live the dream. Fuck Matheus Franke, fuck The ElderNacht and fuck this old cow, he would be rich.

Gerty was confused though. There was not much money in the vault, two or three thousand francs petty cash, a few documents maybe, but the only thing worth stealing, the bearer bonds, were returned last month. Any of the paintings and even the jewellery in her bedside jewellery box would have been worth far more.

The problem was that Anne-Marie was still in the library, still asleep on a sofa just a few metres from the vault. She could not let him in, nor could she watch Mimi murdered.

Another wave of pain hit as the gunman pushed Gerty and Mimi in front of him. Mimi turned around unexpectedly and went for the man.

“No!” Gerty tried to say, but the man had seen it coming a millisecond before and had pistol whipped her across the head for her troubles.

Mimi went down, blood pooling near the wound. Gerty screamed.

“She’s not dead yet, but I will still kill her if you don't get that fucking vault open now!”

They left Mimi unconscious on the floor, and walked to the vault. On her first attempt entering the password on the keypad, she entered the words MATHEUS FRANKE, using the small letters printed over the number on the key. The name would be helpful to Charlie, and trigger the silent alarm if Carl was not already on his way. It was all she could think to delay the inevitable.

As the entry pad rejected the combination and turned red, his fist smashed into the side of her head, knocking her to the floor, a flood of pain hit her again.

“Don't mess with me bitch.” The words were in her head before they were in her ears, “next one and you die.”

He was not joking, he was going to kill her whatever she did, Mimi too and there was nothing she could do to stop him. Her only chance of both of them getting out of here alive was for Carl to get here. She knew Carl would be on his way, and knew from the exercises that they did, that it would be four minutes minimum to arrive at the front door. It was four minutes she knew she did not have, but she could at least buy more time for Mimi and Anne-Marie.

She sensed the new thoughts as Anne-Marie woke up.

She screamed. “Please don't kill me, please don't kill me!” In hope Anne-Marie would hear and hide at least.

[Dragon, tell entity Anne-Marie to hide or she will die!] She forced the thoughts out, not physically able to hear its reply because of the pain.

The man was working on having ten minutes, until some sort of backup arrived and they flew off by helicopter. Knowing his thoughts were only to kill her, she cried and began to enter another wrong code.

Charlie was better and smarter than Gerty, she could feel it, her presence was much more controlled, she felt how she could project small emotions onto people and animals at close range and how restrained she could be in the actual use of the gift. She was very proud of her daughter. Her daughter would find this man and punish him.

[Dragon, tell my daughter that I love her]

her thoughts to the dragon did not surface and fell on deaf ears just as the entry pad turned red again. Almost the same instant the bullet slammed into her shoulder, spun her around and she collapsed onto her knees.

Gerty sensed the spot he was aiming at a second before he pulled the trigger. Her gift did not help, and in this case only enhanced the terror.

* * *

Carl flew through the open gate and moved the speeding Range Rover over the manicured grass verge cutting several seconds off the time it would take if he used the winding gravel track. He timed his stop perfectly on the grass outside the side of the house, assessing the threat before he stopped and jumping out of the car with his gun ready and the safety off.

He glanced through the open window by the door, the gun facing down until a threat was identified, there were no sounds he could detect which helped him make the decision to enter.

His back to the wall, he slowly opened the door and pushed it open, after a quick all clear glance he was in and at the end of the room to the kitchen, he cleared the kitchen and walked through the open kitchen door into the area behind the stairs.

Peering around he saw Mimi, she was unmoving, a small pool of blood to her head, he glanced quickly around, knowing all the places that you could hide and got to her within seconds, it was serious, a blow to the head maybe, definitely concussion, but the weak pulse meant she was alive and he would not move her. His only task now was to find and protect Gerty, that was when he saw the next pool of blood and the trail to the vault.

* * *

She collapsed onto her back, her bloodied hand held against her dress, the tear from the bullet hardly discernible against her pain. From her vast medical knowledge she knew a shot to the stomach was generally fatal and the most painful way to die, but her mind could barely process anything above the waves of pain.

She would never see Charlie again, none of her good friends, and never have grandchildren. She screamed again as an even more intense wave of pain cut into her stomach.

Gerty fell into unconsciousness, the pressure on her stomach fell away as her muscles relaxed and blood oozed between her fingers. Her last thoughts were not even hers, [That was for my father you fucking bitch]

“That was for my father you fucking bitch!” Spat the Priest as he re-aimed the gun at the lock on the room and fired six times.

Carl heard the shots from the library, and ignoring caution, ran towards it, his instincts on shoot to kill rather than disarm. The door was broken open and ajar, Gerty on the floor in a pile of blood unmoving, the man ahead of him just walking through the open door. The man must have felt something and turned.

There would be no warning shot, no request to put down the weapon. The single shot to the head caught the man just as his gun arm was being raised and before he had realised he needed to move out of the way.

Carl’s mouth moved to his microphone. “Hostile dead, two seriously wounded. Immediate evac requested. First victim gunshot wound to the stomach, second victim severe concussion.”

The acknowledgement came back instantly.

“EMT called, backup on site. ETA is five minutes.”

Carl ripped the end of Gerty’s dress off and bunched it into the wound holding the pressure there to stop the bleeding in both her hand and stomach. Gerty was by far the more seriously wounded of the two, he doubted she would survive another ten minutes and all he could really do was watch her die.

A few seconds later he heard the faint sound of the helicopter.

The nearest first aid kit was back in the kitchen but it was no use here, his only job was to stem the blood loss until they arrived.

“Anne-Marie, if you are safe. I need you here now. Gerty is wounded.” He shouted in English loudly.

“She appeared shaking hysterically at the sight of the dead intruder. Then screamed at the sight of Carl over Gerty’s body. Then she looked behind Carl, and fainted.

Carl’s one and only mistake was to assume the helicopter was the backup. He was so concerned with keeping Gerty alive, he never even turned at the sound of the running feet behind him. He wasn't sure of the pressure so held the fabric tightly into the wound, annoyed that Anne-Marie had fainted. The first bullet ripped through his back, the next three his heart.

* * *

Simon diverted the car to the hospital emergency centre and arrived at the same time as the helicopter which they had just seen alighting on the roof. He ignored the very strict no parking signs throwing the car into the first slot he found.

An emergency responder had given him the update over the phone fifteen minutes ago, Carl was dead, both Gerty and his wife were seriously wounded, Gerty was Critical but his wife was stable. He felt terrible that he was feeling relief that his wife was stable, and nobody mentioned Anne-Marie.

Charlie, who was shaking and crying after the message from the emergency responder, threw open the car door the second he stopped, ignoring Ethan and not even bothering to close it as she ran for the hospital entrance.

Nobody stopped her, she knew the layout and ran straight for where her mother would arrive, the sense of fear emanating from her seemingly affecting everyone in the hospital. Simon and Ethan came rushing up behind her, both presences ensured nobody would have second thoughts about stopping them.

She saw her mother being pushed slowly along the corridor, and rushed up grabbing her hand for a tiny fraction of a second before the morose faces, and sad thoughts of the nurse and doctor told her everything. Gerty had died five minutes ago in the helicopter from massive internal bleeding, she had never recovered consciousness.

Charlie collapsed over her mothers still warm body, still holding her limp hand, and her tears streaming out. She stayed like that for another minute, the blood soaking through the sheets and into her blue dress, her tears falling across Mama’s still beautiful, now sleeping face. After five minutes Ethan took her hand and lifted her up. Charlie's face buried into his, as her sobbing continued.

All the family had dropped everything and were en-route to the hospital, all would find they were also minutes too late.

* * *

The doctor and nurse had rushed off to another emergency while she was crying, replaced by an orderly who waited for her patiently. Simon had also left her grieving, going to seek out Mimi.

Two new officials walked up carefully, stopping a respectful distance from Charlie and both horrified by the sight in front of them. Both it seemed knew Gerty well from their restrained outpouring of grief. She picked up that the corridor had been closed off by some policemen further along, and saw the two concerned men were waiting for her. She knew the grieving process would have to wait.

“Please give me a few minutes gentlemen,” she said softly, moving off Ethan’s shoulder and facing away from everyone to wipe her tears with her handkerchief. She turned and nodded softly to the orderly who pushed her mothers trolley away.

She was a mess. Her face was red from crying, her hands and dress soaked with her mothers blood. She recognised the two men, Philippe Moran the Commandant of the police, and one of his assistants Desmond Voizin. A third person had also just arrived, a woman who one of the men identified for her as the head of the Cantonal Civil Protection Service. Several aides waited behind a new police cordon.

The three officials were now talking with Ethan, while she stared back at a wall, wiping her eyes and controlling her breathing. She had nowhere to direct the rage she felt.

[WHY?] she screamed at herself, at the dragon, [WHY?]

The chatter in the corridor halted immediately, the only sound, the single voice in her head.

[I do not understand] came the predictable answer.

Charlie realised she needed to crawl out of her depression, to help find who did this to mother, to Carl and Mimi. At least Anne-Marie had gotten away.. Oh god, CERN! Did she return to work? Charlie had initially picked Anne-Marie up from her lab, which was located under the building that had been destroyed. And what about Michael?

[dragon what is the status of entity Anne-Marie?]

[Last contact with Entity Anne-Marie was twenty seven minutes ago, location seven metres from dragon]

Damn! Anne-Marie was with Gerty and Carl when they were murdered, she must have been taken. Charlie straightened herself up, her rage suppressed, and in full investigation mode. Like a judge she projected control, and the four people waiting for her must have sensed this. Nobody had spoken since Charlie had screamed in her head a few seconds earlier, and when she turned she found everybody giving her their full attention.

Charlie used her phone and dialled a number from memory, a direct number only about a dozen other people had.

“Doctor Schubert, it is negotiator Charlie..” She spoke fast and in German, Ethan noticed the three people watching Charlie and not understanding until the commandant Moran whispered to him in English, "she is talking to the leader of the federal council, basically our president.”

“I appreciate your sympathy, Doctor. It is about that and the murder of the CERN people. Time is of the essence to capture the people who did this. I am going to need the full backing of the committee and may ask the government to make a statement I will prepare for you..”

“I am deadly serious Doctor Schubert.” She said forcefully. “Every second counts and there is a chance we can identify and bring the perpetrators to justice before they get away..”

“I suspect for twenty four hours..”

“As soon as I have reliable information Hr Doctor Schubert..”

“Thank you.”

She hung up and walked over to the four people. She turned to the oldest man, he looked to be in his sixties, shorter than her, slightly framed and balding, with spectacles and a dark grey suit.

“Commandant Moran. Philippe, I will be assisting you with the command of the investigation from the Swiss side. Do you have the list of casualties and missing people at CERN?”

“Er, thank you ma’am, yes let me see.” The man flipped through his clipboard and passed it to her.

Ethan looked on, wondering why the top man in the Geneva police force was deferring to Charlie. He turned to the other man and the new woman next to him for an explanation, but none was given. All were at rapt attention.

“Philippe, there is no mention of Doctor Anne-Marie Verne. Was she signed in?” Said Charlie after a brief glance at the page.

“No ma’am, the list was very thoroughly checked by several people.” The Commandant looked to the man next to him, “Desmond, is this still the latest list?”

“Yes Commandant,” said the second man.

“OK,” she moved her focus on the new man, “Desmond do you know if an Audi e-tron is still parked at the chateau?”

“Ma’am, yes the car was outside your house along with the two older BMW cars used by the terrorists who did this. It is being checked along with everything else.”

“Thank you Desmond, they were not terrorists, they were a group known as The ElderNacht, probably eight to ten men who may already be out of the country. If the car is there, and there is no body, then they may have also abducted a French scientist from CERN called Doctor Anne-Marie Verne who was a guest. The Audi was a Department of Justice VIP car on loan to her, and she is not listed anywhere here. Can you please check for me and bring any information to us as soon as you can.”

The man nodded and walked off with his phone out.

Alice ran up to the group with wet eyes, panting. “Oh Charlie, I am so sorry..”

“There will be time for grieving Alice, I need you at your best now to assist me, and the group here; Can you keep notes and actions so these guys can refer to you without coming to me if necessary?”

“Yes Charlie.” She looked at Charlie, her red eyes and her bloodied dress sympathetically, and Charlie could sense she was grieving for Gerty, and really wanted to hug, and comfort Charlie, but she was also angry, and a professional.

Charlie addressed the commandant, “OK Philippe so as all the cars are still there, they must have left using other means, a boat is unlikely, so more likely a helicopter. We need to find them now as this woman may still be alive.”

“Also Commissioner Moran. I believe we should issue an international arrest warrant for Matheus Franke, a member of The ElderNacht and a German citizen. His name was explicitly contained in my mothers last message to me.”

“I think so too Ma’am, I will do it immediately.”

Another man arrived and the commandant was deep in conversation for a minute before the man quickly moved off.

Charlie turned to the final woman, “Madame Roche?”

“Josephine Ma’am.”

“Josephine, as I have activated amendment three point one, the country has declared a national emergency, you may go ahead and requisition any military assets to help. The defence department will expect it and will defer to me for confirmation. Also can one of your people get information on all flights for a thirty kilometre radius around the house for the last hour. Concentrate specifically on helicopter flights entering or leaving Swiss airspace or unregistered ones. I believe we know where their final destination is, so it is likely the helicopter will need to meet up with a jet, probably at a small provincial airport, someone should be able to get the information.”

Ethan, “I would be grateful if you could use your contacts to check for the same, and also for private flights into Costa Verde with a window starting from now.”

“I can do that Charlie, you are thinking the team left for home?”

She nodded, and looked at everyone.

“Two things are suspicious. Firstly they killed my mother, but did not attempt to kill me, secondly they did not kill Anne-Marie. It is my theory that they believe Anne-Marie is me, and they will keep her alive until either they find she is not me, or she is of no use to them. For that reason I don't want any statements issued on what happened at the house, and nothing that indicates I am helping here.”

“But Ma’am.” Said Philippe..

“A young woman’s life is probably in danger, Philippe,” she said soberly, “if the kidnappers find out I am here, and they are holding a French scientist instead, they will likely kill her immediately. We will carry the mute order for the next twelve hours. It is the least we can do for her, after twelve hours it probably will not matter.”

She turned to Ethan.

“I am assigning agent Curran as the Interpol liaison for this case. He will also liaise with his government on the Fermi-lab staff murdered, it said in the list that five were American citizens. Alice, could you confirm that order with my uncle please and ask him to bring the French, German and Italians in and keep them up to date, this is likely to extend to our neighbours.”

Alice nodded, she looked to be multitasking quite well already, and she had the authority to requisition additional bodies if she needed to, not that she ever did.

Charlie turned to the commandant again, “we should also alert the principal investigators for CERN and the murders at my house, they are to be treated as the same case and intelligence shared, you and Josephine are the lead, I will advise and ensure efficiency. We only have twelve hours!”

Charlie efficiently coordinated the start of the investigation with the authorities. It seemed everyone Ethan had talked to before this had got the negotiator role wrong, the title covered a tiny fraction of the authority she actually had.

Charlie, true to her role, negotiated her way through rather than around the bureaucracy, but only applying pressure where necessary, and giving the people the information to do their jobs.

Ethan had given his short update to the head of the CIA, and, due to the number of Americans killed, and the possible threat to the country, the Joint Chiefs of Staff.

* * *

“It is baked into their constitution Mr President,” said the director of the CIA to the Joint Chiefs of Staff. “I re-read the short report produced by agent Ethan Curran an hour ago and went through it with one of my contacts in the State Department who knows the country.

“Amendment three point two of the Swiss constitution. A negotiator shall hold no power, but will sit in judgement of all those in power. Three point three, a negotiator can be called for fair and neutral arbitration in anything involving the well-being of the state. Three point five, a Negotiator will not dispense any form of justice, but will pass responsibility to the state, it seems the whole amendment three is to do with this position.”

He shook his head. “It then goes on to list the mandatory requirements for a negotiator, including, and I quote, three point seven point one, speak fluently all the languages of the state, its neighbours, and the global powers. Three point seven point two, Understand completely the constitution, statute and laws of the state without nuance. Three point seven point six, no negotiator will be appointed without these abilities being tested by their peers, and the agreement of the majority of the sitting council. And get this, three point seven point eleven the negotiator cannot abdicate their responsibilities but can be replaced by another negotiator or stood down if their judgement is considered flawed by an eighty percent majority vote of the standing government counsel.”

“Hang on a minute,” said the vice president, “you said the negotiator was a twenty year old woman..”

“Twenty four sir.”

“I think what Dan was getting at, Director, and frankly so am I,” said the president, “is how the hell can they have appointed someone so young to such a high position? She can’t possibly be qualified, it just doesn't sound plausible..”

“Oh it is most certainly plausible Fredrick!” Interrupted the voice of a woman who had entered the room a few minutes earlier and was just taking a seat.”

“I know both of these women extremely well, Gerty the senior negotiator was one of my best friends, she spoke at least a dozen languages all totally fluently and was the highest judge in Switzerland for some years. Her abilities were legendary. Her daughter Charlie is even more remarkable and I have absolutely no doubt she would have aced all the mandatory requirements listed without even trying, and if they added a dozen more she would ace them too.”

“Thank you Pamela, good of you to join us even if it is a bit late.”

“I am late because I have been confirming all these with our friend the Swiss Ambassador, and via a phone conference call with the Swiss President. They both also listed another paragraph from their constitution the director did not mention, three point one, that a negotiator can declare a national emergency, and at times of national emergency, the negotiator will not assume power, but will advise those in power, and replace them if they are found deficient in their duty. I think that is what has happened here, and the two men say it is the first time it has ever been invoked.”

“What kind of people would write such a constitution?” Said the vice president aghast.

“I asked the same question,” said Pamela, “but there have only been three negotiators in the country's entire history. The first negotiator was the man who penned the constitution to bring the first thirteen separate states together, a cross between George Washington and Gandhi by all accounts. The prerequisites five hundred years ago were of course much easier, only requiring three languages, and the guy obviously helped pen the constitution and laws so knew them very well. Nobody else, until Gerty, ever passed the job interview.”

“Oh and Charlie, the current negotiator, apparently took over the job from her mother when she was nineteen. Neither of the two men had anything but praise for the two women.”

“And this girl is the reason Switzerland is now requesting a special meeting of the United Nations?”

“Yes I believe so Fredrick.”

The men looked at each other aghast.

Pamela’s countenance changed. “You know I knew Gerty for over forty years since school, never once had she mentioned her position. I had dinner with Charlie only a few weeks ago at my home here, she never mentioned it either, she was always polite and like Gerty played down her abilities. I cannot imagine someone holding such powers and not telling anybody.”

“Yes, that is quite remarkable.” Said the President.

“Sir, I have the draft of the statement on the screen now,” interrupted the aide.

The eleven people in the room stared at the large screen.

*Switzerland has determined conclusively that the atrocities carried out today in Geneva causing the murder of twenty nine people of sixteen different nationalities to be an act of terror carried out by a group known as The ElderNacht.*

*It is our country's intention to seek justice and bring everyone in this organisation complicit in the atrocity to trial at a place and time determined by the international committee.*

*All fungible assets identified as owned by this organisation will be seized, any associate of this organisation will be arrested, and any country helping or sheltering this organisation, or the perpetrators of this atrocity will be considered by Switzerland a rogue nation.*

*The Government of Switzerland asks all nations to join it in condemning this organisation and helping it bring them to justice.*

“Wow! It has been what, three hours, they don't mess around!”

“The statement came with a caveat that it was not for public release for twelve hours as a French citizen is believed to be with them and still in danger. The Swiss have attached to the statement a bunch of documentary evidence, also not for public issue, which seems pretty conclusive Sir,” said the aide.

“The organisation is headquartered in the Island nation of Costa Verde, Sir,” said the CIA director, “that paragraph regarding any country sheltering them is directly aimed at their government. Pressure is going to be on them now to take these guys into custody. However it is our assessment that The ElderNacht organisation is far more powerful than the small police force the government of Costa Verde controls.”

“Sir,” said Pamela, “I don't think the US needs to jump in all guns blazing on this one. Let the Swiss control the narrative for the time being.”

“How many Americans killed?” Said the president.

“Five sir.” Said an aide.

The president shook his head sternly.

“There were eight French people also murdered, and one abducted sir,” said the CIA Director, “and the Island is surrounded by French speaking colonies and Islands, I suspect they will be even more keen to go after this organisation.”

“I believe the French government is having the same meeting we are now,” said the vice president.

“OK,” said the president, “somebody go through that evidence and check with our liaison guy, er Mr..”

“Curran sir.”

“Mr Curran, yes, check with him. For now they seem to have it under control but let's reconvene before we issue anything public, however let the Swiss know in private they have our full support if they need it. And I hate to say this, but the same goes for the French.”

* * *

Matheus was content. The operation went off like clockwork and he lost nobody in his team, it was the best possible result. It looked like that idiot The Priest had tried to rob the woman first, but was shot for his troubles. His distraction of the bodyguard though really had made this a smooth ending for him. Matheus had actually been looking forward to killing The Priest himself, he hated pretenders, a stupid fucking name for a stupid little nobody.

The young negotiator woman had woken up just the once, screaming while being transferred to the private jet. But they had gagged her and they knocked her out for the flight, the drugs should last at least until she was at the island.

Yes father will be pleased. All the objectives were completed and he was going to rub it in the face of that mongrel half-brother of his Ahmed, and do it in front of father and the others. He smiled, father was not going to last that much longer, and once he was gone Matheus was the most senior. And then of course Ahmed, his born-again religious nutjob brother would be history.

He watched as one of his men pulled the cute Italian stewardess down onto his lap, then laughed as she struggled, slapped him and ran away to the back of the plane crying. He didn't care, the men had done their job, let them have their fun. The plane was owned by some drug dealer playboy, another nobody who thought he was a big shot because he could drop fifteen million on a private jet so he could impress his equally pretentious playboy friends. The world was full of them.

“Do we have any further updates?” Matheus grunted at one of the guys holding a laptop.

“No sir, this crappy plane does not have satellite internet, and my satellite phone does not work inside. We could radio but it would be in the clear. Last update we had was when we took off, but it was just the latest update on the death count at CERN and nothing yet about the murders at the house. They must be holding that back..”

Matheus smiled, “probably pissed that we have their precious negotiator woman. At least when father has finished with her she will be a bit better looking than the usual two dollar Island whores.”

The men laughed.

* * *

It had been five hours since her mother died. The longest five hours of Charlie’s life, she could not fault the people doing their jobs, when genuine bottlenecks and bureaucracy reared their heads she immediately severed through them, only two people had been stood down. The ElderNacht had left a bloody and shoddy trail that the police investigators had easily followed, and the investigators had already pieced together most of the puzzle.

Michael and Anne-Marie’s other friend Julianne, the cleaner, had still not been found, the six unaccounted for CERN people were all believed to be in the underground lab when it collapsed and considerable effort was now underway to find and rescue any survivors.

There still had been no real time for grief for Mama or Carl, and Mimi’s condition was unchanged. Charlie would have returned home, but it was still a crime scene and she could do no good there. Dragon was safely locked in her vault but she resisted the urge to talk to it for fear of becoming too tired to assist. She limited herself to essential communication.

[Dragon, inform Anne-Marie that she needs to pretend to be me, it doesn't matter what they say, she needs to pretend to be me. Use those words exactly]

[Let me know when she is in contact again]

She waited but no answer.

[Acknowledge the last two communications] she thought impatiently.

[acknowledged the last two communications from entity Charlie]

God this thing was so frustrating at times.

[Can you relay our thoughts?]

[Yes, that is one of my roles. Entity Anne-Marie is out of contact]

[dragon. Can I communicate with the other dragon?]

[yes]

[how do I address it?]

[you cannot, you must touch it for it to know you, or communicate through me]

[can you give it my identification without touching it?]

[yes, that is possible]

Goddamn, for something so advanced it was incredibly annoying to work with. She wondered if alien computer systems were deliberately useless or she was just using a primitive way to communicate with it.

[can you send my identification to the other dragon and tell it to open communication with me?]

[I have initiated the second dragon wake up procedure and the identification transfer, however to communicate you must be within the required range for communication directly. Outside that range communication must be through this unit]

[I am outside your range yet I can talk to you, how is this possible?]

[this dragon is] [...] [entity Charlie is] [...] of [...]

Charlie wondered if any of the other women had this problem, she also wondered for the first time, why only women. And why do the men die..

[dragon why can you only communicate with females?]

[I can communicate with every one who has the correct] [...] [...]

[but all the entities have been female]

It did not answer, did she need to make it a question, god she was getting tired, what was it talking to dragon!

[acknowledge god damn it!]

[acknowledge all the human entities have been females..]

Ethan tapped her and handed over his iPad, his thoughts almost impossible to discern in the crowded hospital room, but his voice clear.

“A Bombardier global express Jet left a small airport in Italy four hours ago bound for Bermuda, but has since diverted to Venezuela. Its current course however puts it en route for Costa Verde.”

The map of the Atlantic on the iPad showed dozens of planes tracking with one in red highlighted.

“Can it reach the island without refuelling?”

“Unfortunately, if it has a full tank then yes.”

“Who owns the plane?”

“It is registered to a shell company in Malta, Interpol shows the owner has links with drug smuggling.”

“That’s probably it. Do you have any idea if we can intercept it?”

“It will arrive at Costa Verde in about four hours. Fighter jets from Florida could probably intercept it and maybe the British or French have something closer, but if they did, any attempt at forcing it down might kill Anne-Marie.”

“Thank you Ethan I need to remember my limitations, you are right to point that out.”

She broke off to update Alice and the government. She knew one way or another this would be over within the next day or two.

# Island of Terror
